#### Follow-on: store-issue repair and first nonblocking L2 increment

**OoO store-issue deadlock repaired.** `mem_stall_i` (= `md_stall || older_st`)
gated STORE, but a store allocates its LSQ entry at dispatch and `older_st` is
derived from that entry, so the store blocked its own issue→AGU→WB path — the only
path that resolves it. `g6lc_iq` now gates LOAD only. Six live-path records pass
(ALU control, repaired store, two stores, load-ordering guard) with `DISPATCH_ID`
and `DISPATCH_LOAD_ORDER` controls failing as required. Load conservatism is
deliberately untouched: relaxing it would open a real hole, because `stl_stall` is
excluded from issue select to avoid a combinational loop. Holding stores until
commit was analysed and rejected for now — without a monotonic age it creates a
new deadlock (older load behind a younger uncommitted store).

**L2 same-line hit-under-miss landed.** The controller was structurally
single-transaction, so merging could never occur and `waiter_pop_i` was tied low.
Readers to the line being filled are now accepted during `S_MISS_AR/R/INSTALL`,
parked as MSHR waiters, and drained from the filled line with their own id and
beats; completion moved past the response. The waiter payload stores the in-line
offset only — the full-address form cost +634 flops at depth 2 versus +170, for no
added capability, which is why it was replaced before landing.

Measured, leaf fixture: 8 readers of one shared line 50 → **42 cycles**; 8 readers
of 8 distinct lines **176 → 176** (control). The gain is removal of
request-acceptance serialisation, *not* DRAM-traffic reduction — the baseline
already absorbed later same-line readers as post-install hits. Area at the
promoted depth two: +301 cells, +170 flops.

Qualification: 12 hit-under-miss records including five injected controls, with
merge engagement observed directly; the pre-change build reproduces
`HUM_NOT_ENGAGED`; 32/32 existing L2 records phase-identical against a *matched*
baseline build; MSHR proofs re-run unchanged and still pass; 54/54 component and
24/24 integration records.

Two harness defects were found and fixed rather than worked around: the
integration overlay list silently omitted `g6lc_l2_top.sv`, so the first pass of
that gate proved nothing about the L2 change; and the L2 bench keyed its install
monitor on `mshr_complete` as a proxy for the tag write, which deferred completion
invalidated. The integration gate now requires cookie plus architectural identity
and records exact cycle identity separately — all cookies are unchanged, and the
11 stream8 records differ only in retirement-cycle annotations (verified by
diffing 80,667 identical trace lines).

**No end-to-end speedup is claimed** for either change: every SMT2 and stream8
cookie is identical. Remaining: real nonblocking needs concurrent fills with
response routing/reordering; full OoO still needs LSQ age/lifetime, rename
admission/recovery, hart/FP ownership and wide retirement.

#### 2. Decision method: preserve contracts, rank removable cost

Apply `AGENTS-coding-philosophy.md` §2.10 and
`AGENTS-rt-learning-philosophy-AGI.md` before selecting an implementation:

1. Name the useful-work objective, exact configuration and observed boundary.
2. Separate **measured**, **structural**, and **hypothesized** findings. A plausible
   source-level trigger is not a reproduced integration failure.
3. Write the contract, competing explanation and cheapest discriminating check.
   Trace accepted work by owner, transaction ID and allocation generation.
4. Stabilize the observer and its positive/negative controls. Freeze the source,
   workload, service model, comparison set and acceptance rules before evaluating.
5. Change one functional mechanism per candidate. Review its complete diff before
   further integration; retain failed candidates and counterexamples.
6. Rank by demonstrably removable stalls/logic, qualification burden and reuse.
   Treat this as a qualitative ordering, not an invented probability or speedup.
   Correctness, permissions and required stability gates cannot be weighted away.

Frequency stays at the inferred 1.25 GHz target of record. Reduced cycles are not
an Fmax claim. Current `physicalDesign.flow` is `none`; approved library/SRAM,
corner, clock and activity inputs are needed for mapped area, STA and power.
Structural FO4 screens are useful for rejecting cones, not proving chip speed.

#### 3. Ranked tracks and concrete improvement targets

Tracks may move in priority when a discriminator changes the evidence. Single-core
research may proceed independently of a blocked multicore gate, but no failing
shared-path prerequisite may be hidden by an aggregate throughput score.

| Track / priority | Current evidence | Suggested target, not a promised gain | Gate before retention |
|---|---|---|---|
| A0 — protect retained gains | Measured and source-bound | Preserve IQ/corrector benefits and named SMT2 integer pass; close no more scope than tested | Matching source/config/ELF/runtime plus relevant tests; no stale model adoption |
| A1 — single-core warm-fetch throughput | Structural limit with a matching workload observation | Investigate warm request initiation interval 2 → 1 while preserving hit latency and transaction identity | Independent request/response/kill conservation; no combinational valid/ready loop; minimal/RVI/RVC/SMT2 checks and timing/cell comparison |
| A2 — multicore transport/coherence stability | Invalidation leaf and four hub address/ID/credit cases repaired; accepted-write notification loss still fails; inclusive/integrated qualification open | Stable held AXI payload/ID and lossless accepted invalidations under backpressure | Reproduce each scenario; per-target obligations and selected-source acknowledgments proved; real concurrent-worker data checks |
| A3 — cheaper serialized L2 control | Depth 2 promoted in SMT2/stream8 after production-shaped and matched-package gates; cycles/traces unchanged | Retain measured area benefit without growing concurrency or changing other defaults | Preserve dual-hart references; generic MSHR and other packages stay unchanged; physical/platform/coherence gates remain open |
| A4 — CPU-visible memory service and cacheability | Prior cacheable scan regression; cheap class-0 memory; retained predictor changes the baseline | Rebuild only the missing matched leg, then compare bypass/cacheable traffic under declared request latency and backpressure | Correct bytes/IDs/fences/invalidation first; no execute-map change or automatic workaround removal |
| A5 — L2 tag storage | Resettable flop tags, SRAM-backed data | Study moving tag payload to synchronous SRAM with separately initialized validity, without adding a hot-hit stage if feasible | Port schedule, invalidation/fill race and reset/DFT contract; actual macro views before area claims |
| A6 — predictor completeness / useful gating | PH_BHT legacy PC path; shared per-window predictor behavior; way-prediction gating needs an audit | Align provider/slot semantics and remove measured redundant reads/retries before growing tables | Opposite outcomes in one window, RVC carry, aliasing, train-vs-lookup history and disabled-path controls |
| A7 — measured SMT efficiency / architectural completion | Integer pass; unbanked FPR and context-ownership questions remain | Measure switch/refetch cost and fairness before scheduler changes; close advertised FP/context isolation separately | Preserve integer reference gate; explicit FP/CSR/MMU/atomic/WFI tests; no register/PC-specific exceptions |
| A8 — concurrency, width, prefetch expansion | Existing declared capacity exceeds proven live service | Two independently owned read misses/hit-under-miss only if measured; T=1/I=2 before I=4; real prefetch destination before depth growth | A2/A4 contracts, full transaction/error/cancel handling, protected-workload results and mapped cost |

##### A1: the next single-core discriminator

`g6lc_icache.sv:292-367` accepts in IDLE, delivers a warm hit in READ, and does not
accept another request in READ. `build_config_pkg.sv:8-16,294-299` gives stream8
one issue port and a 32-bit fetch window; FTQ/FDIP/loop buffer are disabled in its
package. The uncompressed checked loop has nine instructions and takes
`9 × 2 × 8,192 + 14 = 147,470` ROI cycles. This is consistent with reaching the
current supply ceiling after the persistent branch miss was repaired.

A one-window-per-cycle interface is therefore a justified **design target**.
Its RVI supply bound is up to 2×, not a predicted CPU speedup; compressed density,
LSU/issue stalls, branch recovery and memory misses determine actual gains.
First measure accepted windows, IQ-empty cycles, useful issue and backpressure on
straight-line RVI, dense RVC and mixed code with the current pipeline unchanged.

If supply is binding, design bounded registered request/response ownership or
elastic buffering. Settle the exact credit and cancellation interface before
coding. Do **not** simply restore `ready` in READ: earlier versions created a
feedback loop and response/address association defects. Include simultaneous
hit/new request, stalled response, refill, replay, split target, flush/mispredict,
hart switch and non-idempotent/fence cases. No cache-policy or predictor-capacity
change in this candidate. Budget buffer bits and added mux depth explicitly.

##### A2: multicore readiness is not inferred from the SMT2 pass

The protected SMT2 package has one physical core; the coherence hub takes its
identity path. Stream8 has two physical cores, but bypass-heavy/one-worker tests
can hide coherence faults. Preserve the five hub reproducers above and extend
these contract checks; inclusive-source cases still need their own reproduction:

- `g6lc_coherence_hub.sv:120-136,169-195,249-285`: winners and free-slot IDs are
  recomputed while downstream can stall. Hold AR/AW valid with ready low, introduce
  a higher-priority requester or free a lower ID slot, and check payload/ID stability.
  A future fix must retain the selected owner **and** reserved memory-side ID.
- `:187-195`: distinct-slot selection can deny AW when only one slot is free even
  if no AR request needs it. Test that case before increasing the shared four-slot
  limit. The source comment's per-direction wording does not make slots independent.
- `:206-208,244-270,513+`: AW acceptance does not reserve invalidation capacity.
  Saturate a targeted consumer and prove every accepted write's obligations are
  delivered or validly coalesced. Do not create an AW/invalidation combinational loop
  by naively wiring ready back into grant.
- The original `g6lc_inval_bus.sv:59-112` allowed one target's coalescing to assert
  global ready while another target was full with a different line. This state
  is now reproduced and repaired; retain the per-target delivery regression. The hub's source-excluded, multiple-target case
  needs at least three physical cores; it is not demonstrated by N=2 alone.
  Also check coalesce-plus-pop and changing invalidation flags: a merge must not
  erase an obligation that the departing head did not actually deliver.
- `g6lc_cluster.sv:110-115,259-261,384-403` and `g6lc_l3_inclusive_inv.sv:44-65`:
  check simultaneous hub/inclusive requests, acknowledgment only of the selected
  source, and a second eviction while one is pending. L3 is off in the protected
  SMT2/stream8 packages, so this is an L3-promotion prerequisite, not their observed
  failure. Current always-ready consumers also limit backpressure exposure.

Start with isolated interconnect/invalidation tests. Use registered reservation
and pending-obligation designs only after reproducing the relevant violation;
finite queues cannot provide lossless service by declaring traffic best-effort.
Do not combine a coherence fix, tag-SRAM conversion and nonblocking L2 in one diff.

##### A3–A5: make existing memory hardware useful and cheaper

`g6lc_l2_top.sv:199-227,350-545` serializes acceptance through response completion,
leaves waiter drain disconnected and completes an MSHR before serving its response.
First assert/measure live demand occupancy. A depth-two serialized candidate avoids
both the depth-one `$clog2(DEPTH)-1:0` port issue in `g6lc_l2_mshr.sv:28-35` and
changing a previously never-full status into a continuously asserted one. Do not
promise savings until paired synthesis shows retained logic actually disappears.
Keep data-bank mapping and SRAM geometry fixed for that comparison.

Before any nonblocking implementation, address merge-full acceptance in
`g6lc_l2_mshr.sv:98-101,135-146` and the top's live-bus `alloc_id_i` rather than
captured request ID. They may be dormant in serial service; do not wire unused
waiters and call it concurrency. Target correct ownership of two read misses,
then hit-under-miss, only if the measured service limit justifies the added state.

For a 256 KiB / eight-way / 64-byte-line cache on a 64-bit address interface,
`g6lc_l2_tag.sv` represents 200,704 tag bits plus 4,096 valid bits. Moving tag
payload from resettable flops to SRAM is a substantial structural opportunity,
not 204,800 bits of measured physical savings. Exploit the serial controller's
accepted-AR stage for synchronous tag-read scheduling where possible; keep lookup,
victim probe, install and invalidation consistent. `l2_back_inval_ready_o` is now
always one and its cluster acknowledgment is ignored: a single-port conversion
cannot silently add stalls or lose invalidations. Do not truncate address tags
without an end-to-end address-range/aliasing proof for every master.

The CPU currently reaches class-0 AXI-to-SRAM with zero added delayer delay;
`G6LC_AI_DRAM_TIMING` affects island DMA, not CPU loads. The existing checked
pointer loop's validation arithmetic can hide short data latency. Define a
minimal dependent-load ROI with independently validated results if latency,
rather than checked-work throughput, is the question. For latency sensitivity,
state whether added delay affects command acceptance, first response or each beat.
Use existing `verif/tb/l2/tb_g6lc_l2.sv`, `tb_g6lc_l2_units.sv` and remote runners
before creating new collateral. Recheck refill ID, RRESP/error propagation and
short/late RLAST behavior (`g6lc_l2_top.sv:482-545`), including the obligation not
to install erroneous data. Nominal-data passes do not qualify those fault paths.

Retest cacheability on a **matched post-predictor baseline** before transferring
old ratios. HPDCACHE execute-region bypass (`cva6_hpdcache_if_adapter.sv:81-96`)
and WT `AxCACHE` allocation (`axi_shim.sv:261`, `g6lc_l2_pkg.sv:38-39`) are distinct
contracts. Keep RR off. The old +35.77% cacheable scan regression is evidence
against automatic enablement, not a universal result for all future pipelines.

#### 4. Measurement and preservation gates

- Keep N=1/T=1 latency, N=1/T=2 SMT efficiency, and N>1/T=1 multicore throughput
  separate. Begin multicore comparisons at N=1 and N=2; N=4 waits for its shared
  path tests. Do not use the unqualified N=4/T=2/I=4 server package as the baseline.
- Record total checked work per global ROI cycle, each worker's completion time,
  slowest-worker behavior, useful issue/retirement and accepted shared-interface
  events. Shared PMU views are not additive. `g6lc_cluster.sv:219` exports core-0
  RVFI only; collect per-core observations explicitly before claiming scaling.
- Separate fixed-total-cache scaling from fixed-cache-per-core scaling. Automatic
  L2 sizing changes with core count; record effective values and deliberate overrides.
  Account separately for APU/firmware-reserved physical cores. A faster core can
  expose shared contention and reduce scaling efficiency while total throughput
  still rises; evaluate both, not just the scaling ratio.
- For SMT, report each hart's work and solo reference before weighted-speedup or
  fairness claims. Quantum/starvation are fetch-handshake/eligible-cycle mechanisms,
  not unconditional time bounds. Keep the existing 128/64 settings until measured.
- Preserve the current dual-active RVI/mixed-C oracle, both 48 KiB/hart workloads,
  redirect-chain and source-bound observer controls whenever shared core/fetch/cache
  behavior changes. A later timing optimization may change trace timestamps and
  polling spins, never required instruction/operand/memory results.
- Retain the corrected private runtime and compiler-dependency checks. Use the
  `build-platform/.remote-ssh-creds` gateway, immutable run directories and existing
  native simulation recipes. Do not copy objects across variants or use stale
  cached outputs. No local Windows/WSL hardware simulation as replacement evidence.
- Run the narrow changed-contract tests first, then one matched integration gate.
  Use applicable lint/elaboration/synthesis and legal-config checks; distinguish
  generic cells, state bits, SRAM dimensions, mapped area, STA and energy.
- Publish every predeclared case, including regressions. Use a Pareto decision over
  qualified workloads; no geometric mean may conceal a correctness failure or a
  protected-case regression. Define uncertainty and trade-off budgets before runs.
  Exact percentage targets are not imposed without a workload/service baseline.

#### 5. Execution order and stop decisions

1. **Done:** confirm scoped promotion and current source identities; preserve all
   existing benefits and proof records without rebuilding unchanged models.
2. **Next small pass:** add the missing cycle-level IQ/readiness/stall observations
   before fetch overlap, and advance held-AXI/producer-reservation checks beyond
   the repaired invalidation leaf. Resolve source-bound remote platform verification
   before broader promotion. Integrate only one functional change at a time.
3. **Done:** A3 depth-two production-geometry and matched-package qualification,
   followed by scoped package promotion. Next keep fetch overlap and invalidation
   visibility as separate candidates; choose using the next contract evidence.
4. Run matched CPU-visible service/cacheability curves and the tag-port/macro study.
   A missing technology bundle blocks physical claims, not independent RTL analysis.
5. Only then consider L2 overlap, T=1 two-wide execution, scheduler tuning or additional
   predictor/prefetch capacity when their measured limiting mechanism warrants it.

Wider retirement is not already available: `scoreboard.sv:475-478` counts both
commits only for exactly two ports, while `commit_stage.sv:178-458` handles ports
zero and one. Preserve the scope of the existing two-port pass; qualify any wider
path before making throughput claims. FPR banking and translation/privilege
ownership remain architectural completion work, not optional performance trades.
`issue_read_operands.sv:1451-1466` has no hart index on its FPR instance; the CSR
bank still exposes active-hart context. Trace ownership before changing either.

For every admitted change, update the existing `AGENTS-todo.md`, spec/test maps,
affected architecture guide and evidence ledger. Use the runtime-learning segment
card: mechanism, evidence, rival prediction, cheapest check, result and applicability
boundary. Stop on a required correctness failure, ambiguous measurement, missing
authority or unsupported implementation interface. Do not hide failures with
firmware edits, added exemptions, lower standards or an altered benchmark goal.

**Current action boundary:** the user has requested promotion of the established
optimizations and this plan reassessment. The targets above are staged candidates,
not blanket approval to implement all of them, change clock/SKU defaults or publish
release-qualified claims. No commit or push is implied by RTL promotion.

## Historical archive — original measurement/L2 plan and experiments (superseded)

Everything below is retained for provenance, not as the active work queue.
In particular, old RR-first priorities and prohibitions predating subsequent user
approvals no longer control the sequence. Claims that VT1 alone made the whole
core clean, or that an RR comparison uniquely established a simulator cause,
were superseded by the host-runtime overwrite discovery and independent fetch/
retirement repairs. Uncached bypass is not a measured 100% cache-miss rate;
I-cache traffic can still exercise L2, so RR is not globally functionally dead.
Execute-region overlays alter the experiment contract and their ratios do not
establish production cacheability gains. Use the current evidence above instead.

Original title: **Balanced LibreCore optimization: measurement foundation and first L2 win**

Establish trustworthy single-core and multicore measurements, preserve named SMT baselines, and qualify one optional L2 replacement improvement before approving wider architectural changes.

### Summary
The user selected **balanced efficiency** and **foundation plus first win**. The immediate implementation tranche will establish reliable evidence and short performance experiments, then evaluate an optional L2 replacement change. It will not attempt simultaneous cache-concurrency, frontend, SMT, and OoO rewrites. Single-core latency and multicore throughput remain separate objectives; architectural correctness and SMT non-regression are hard constraints, not terms that can be traded for performance.

No source files, project documentation, configuration, or remote state were changed during this planning pass. The source findings below are read-only observations, not fresh simulation/formal results. The observed starting commit was `ed6c74236`. Recheck the worktree before implementation: there are concurrent user changes in APU, BIOS, QEMU, the remote proxy, licensing tiers, and traceability documents.

### Decisions and boundaries
- Retain the 1.25 GHz target-of-record as an inferred planning point, not a timing-closure assertion. No frequency, voltage, process, power-budget, or production-target changes in this tranche.
- Preserve the existing named packages and the B/legacy fetch distinction. Do not merge stream8, SMT2, OoO server, RVV, AI or APU configurations.
- First candidate: **invalid-first, per-set round-robin L2 replacement**, off by default. It is a candidate, not a promised speedup. Do not copy the existing L1 RRIP implementation into L2 or enable RRIP without a separate measurement/verification decision.
- Keep L2 request scheduling, banking, memory map, line size, write policy and L3 policy unchanged in the first candidate.
- Preserve SMT scheduler thresholds, boot exclusions, WT fixup settings, predictor choices, issue barriers, issue width, FP/CSR state and OoO enable settings. Correctness fixes to those belong to separate, targeted follow-ons.
- Default developer smoke behavior stays available, but strict qualification must distinguish skips/artifact checks from executed RTL tests. No waiver/baseline weakening, fake PASS, stale harness adoption, or acceptance of an expected-fail test as release evidence.
- All Verilator RTL evidence and all SMT/Spike evidence run through the existing remote proxy. No local Windows/WSL hardware runs. Do not provision tools, synchronize remote sources, delete artifacts, or start builds until implementation is approved and the relevant permissions are satisfied.
- Keep concurrent APU work separate. `apu_soc_legal()` currently requires `NrCores >= 2` and `NrHarts == 1` for an assigned firmware hart. Record reserved service-core capacity separately from application-core throughput; do not turn an SMT sibling into APU firmware capacity.

### Current-state findings that shape the work
1. **Issue width is not retirement capability.** `g6lc64_ooo_server` requests four issue/commit ports, but `scoreboard.sv:474-490` counts two commits only for `NrCommitPorts==2`; otherwise it counts port zero. `commit_stage.sv` implements the retirement decisions for ports zero and one. Wider retirement remains a completion prerequisite, not an available performance baseline.
2. **SMT preservation requires ownership, not merely retained PCs.** Integer banks exist, but FPR accesses are unbanked in `issue_read_operands.sv:1408-1466`; CSR translation/privilege outputs are selected by the active fetch hart in `g6lc_smt_csr_bank.sv:453-490`. Drain-across-switch, mixed-hart commit effects, translation contexts and FP isolation require dedicated qualification before general SMT release. First-tranche performance work does not modify them.
3. **L2 has storage for MSHRs but a serial service path.** `g6lc_l2_top.sv:186-255,345-531` leaves waiter drain unused and walks one request through its controller. Increasing MSHR parameters alone is not a valid concurrency experiment.
4. **Replacement is first-invalid, otherwise way zero.** `g6lc_l2_top.sv:414-437`. This is a small, isolated experiment with a plausible capacity benefit. Round-robin can also evict useful lines that fixed-way replacement happened to preserve, so adverse and held-out traces are mandatory.
5. **L2 banking is way-influenced.** `g6lc_l2_data.sv:45-70` computes bank from `(set * associativity + way) % banks`. For eight ways and four banks, the bank is determined by the way modulo four; it is not the documented simple `addr[7:6]` mapping. Do not claim L2/DRAM-channel bank alignment or change either mapping without an explicit later experiment.
6. **L3 reuses the L2 engine.** `g6lc_l3_top.sv:39-69`. Keep the engine's new option default-off and bind L3 explicitly to the legacy policy so L2 experiments cannot silently alter L3.
7. **Coherence must be qualified before faster traffic.** AW acceptance in `g6lc_coherence_hub.sv:244-270` is independent of invalidation readiness. `g6lc_inval_bus.sv:59-112` aggregates coalescing across targets. The inclusive path also needs selected-source acknowledgment and pending-eviction backpressure review (`g6lc_cluster.sv:101-106,374-387`, `g6lc_l3_inclusive_inv.sv:44-65`). Reproduce these classes before changing concurrency. A failing relevant prerequisite blocks multicore promotion, not the isolated experiment.
8. **The server prefetcher is not a completed cache-fill mechanism.** `g6lc_server_prefetcher.sv:159-187` blocks demand AR while a PF is outstanding and absorbs its returned data. Leave it unchanged/off in the first candidate; later work must define a real destination and demand QoS.
9. **Evidence-selection gap.** `verify.ts:209-217,272-299` selects lint/synth targets separately from sim suites. `runner.ts` records rc/ok/skip but not DUT/evidence strength. `ooo-l3-tests.sh` can pass a lint fallback; `stability-regress.sh` can finish with skipped required legs.
10. **Provenance gap.** The optional proxy output-cache key uses tracked sources and omits some build options; generated bootrom is produced later. New untracked HDL and consumed generated inputs must participate in qualification identity. Hash actual inputs, not just Git HEAD.
11. **Formal non-vacuity gap.** The rename harness defaults to PRF=16 despite a 32-entry architectural map, and truncates the bound to its address width, excluding active operation. Repair the harness and require reachable non-reset covers before interpreting any proof result. Do not assume away failures in the live DUT.
12. **Existing workloads are functional controls, not performance coverage.** `mc_stream_plane.S` works on 512 bytes; `l3_stride_stream.S` explicitly describes a 16 KiB functional test. Neither establishes LLC capacity benefit, saturation, or concurrent-worker throughput.
13. **Existing synthesis smoke is not area sign-off.** `eda.ts:synthTarget` currently runs `read_slang; hierarchy; proc; opt -fast; check; stat`. Label that as elaboration/RTL-structure smoke. Add a separate paired leaf synthesis report for cell comparisons, with memory mapping disclosed.
14. **QEMU is a search/diagnosis instrument.** CPU D2 currently includes dimensions and synthetic counters rather than calibrated pipeline/SMT timing. Existing native checkpoint support is not a general multicore RTL importer. Preserve those fidelity boundaries.

### Implementation Steps — first approved tranche

#### P0 — Freeze trustworthy baselines and reconcile architectural truth
**Goal:** establish what can be compared without altering CPU behavior.

1. Capture a provenance manifest for the actual selected DUT: source contents including consumed untracked/generated files, expanded flists/includes, parameter/define overrides, effective `CVA6Cfg`, tool versions, executable digest, firmware/ELF/DTB/input hashes, run seed, memory-service model and enabled harts. Regenerated bootrom must be hashed after generation and before building the executable.
2. Extend the existing host suite/result contract with actual target/top, evidence kind, terminal result and artifact locations. Evidence kinds are explicit (`artifact`, `iss`, `rtl-leaf`, `rtl-core`, `rtl-cluster`, `linux`) rather than one implied ranking. Formal depth/mode/cover reachability and physical evidence are separate fields.
3. Add an opt-in strict qualification mode to the existing `verify` command, backed by named target-to-required-suite mappings in `schema.ts/defaults.ts`. In strict mode, `--target` must resolve the matching required simulation suite set or refuse; it must not run unrelated default smoke and claim that target passed. Required skip, dry-run, lint fallback, missing final marker, stale/mismatched target, timeout, transport uncertainty or missing reports cannot qualify.
4. Preserve existing developer defaults; annotate their evidence honestly. Retrofit only the relevant wrappers (`stability-regress.sh`, `ooo-l3-tests.sh`, new L2/perf suite) rather than rewriting the full regression catalog.
5. Extend the proxy's existing build/run provenance path and result parsing, preserving the user's edits. Include actual options/tool versions and consumed untracked/generated input contents. Do not use cached outputs lacking a matching manifest. Avoid broadening private-file sync. Preserve and separately report simulator rc and transport rc; classify test completion from full logs and explicit contracts.
6. Use immutable per-run source snapshots for new leaf experiments through proxy `py --data ... --pull`, following the source-hashed, positive/negative-marker approach in `ai-gemm-reuse.py`, without making scalar tests import AI-specific code. Use isolated configuration-specific Mdirs; no default-harness overwrite or cross-flavour object copying.
7. Repair rename formal harness defaults/range comparisons and add separate cover tasks. Use a legal PRF size, a defined reset/deassertion sequence, and independent legal allocation/free/WB stimulus constraints. Require covers for allocation, dual allocation, exhaustion and recovery. Newly exposed RTL failures become explicit follow-up tests; do not fold an OoO rewrite into P0 or report BMC as unbounded proof.
8. Reconcile affected existing architecture/guide/status rows now: requested parameters versus observed paths, serial L2 versus allocated MSHRs, way-based banking, current single-issue stream envelope, actual CVXIF mutex settings, and historical boot-payload identity. Preserve prior records with their provenance instead of deleting history or claiming new failures were reproduced.

**P0 exit:** positive/negative classifier tests pass; actual DUT/config identity is checked; required tests are either genuinely executed or explicitly blocked; no tooling-only result is mistaken for RTL qualification.

#### P1 — Minimal measurements and failure-discriminating tests
**Goal:** get useful answers from small deterministic runs before full-core rebuilds.

1. Add a dedicated parameterized L2 leaf TB using real `g6lc_l2_top/tag/data/mshr` and an AXI memory responder with deterministic latency/backpressure and explicit error-injection cases. No complete existing L2 leaf performance surface was found, so this new TB is necessary. Reuse existing AXI types/macros and `tc_sram` rather than invent interfaces.
2. Run baseline and candidate instances against the same request sequence using independent ready/valid drivers and identical memory initialization. Do not make the faster instance wait for the slower one; compare ordered responses/checksums and each instance's own elapsed cycles.
3. Add full-set turnover, displaced-hot-working-set reuse, conflict streams, scan-plus-hot-line, random bounded working sets, multiple independent streams, write/read integrity and back-invalidation cases. Include patterns favoring the baseline; retain separate held-out address permutations/working-set transitions. Leaf address generation derives set-conflict strides from geometry, not arbitrary firmware addresses.
4. Test handshakes: held AR/AW, response backpressure, simultaneous read/write pressure, legal bursts, noncacheable and exclusive bypass, same-address write/read sequences, reset and back-invalidation. Cover relevant existing error paths; if the baseline violates the contract, retain the failure and stop promotion rather than exclude it from claims.
5. Add focused invalidation producer/consumer tests before multicore promotion: full target queue, one target coalescing while another is full, and simultaneous hub/inclusive sources. Do not change the coherence protocol in this tranche unless a separately reviewed, narrowly scoped prerequisite fix is approved.
6. Add opt-in simulation-only core metrics at the testbench boundary. Count accepted non-dropped retirements by instruction hart tag for every observed physical core; distinguish traps/cancelled slots. Keep the existing trace format stable and add a structured sidecar rather than changing legacy text parsers. Do not claim multicore IPC from the current core-0-only RVFI output.
7. Define per-workload begin/end/result markers and input checksums. Markers are testbench/software instrumentation, never production-RTL address/opcode exceptions. Measure warmup separately, then steady-state and cold-inclusive totals. For the first tranche, use read-mostly pointer/array kernels and scalar/stream integrity controls. Large write-intensive or SMP cases depend on their correctness gates.
8. Generate a test-local config overlay in the isolated build directory: replace exactly the selected config-package path in a derived flist, change only allowlisted fields, record both base and effective values, and validate them after elaboration. Do not edit checked-in packages to toggle experiments. Extend the existing builder's derived-flist path for this explicit overlay; compare identical base+overlay sets except the candidate bit. Bare-metal N=1/N=2 variants are labelled experimental, not new Linux SKUs.

**Metrics contract:**
- `cycles`: actual RTL clock edges over the declared ROI; never QEMU synthetic time.
- `work`: completed, checked operations/bytes/requests; partial completion is failure.
- `retired`: non-dropped instructions, attributed per physical core/local hart; any architectural counters used must be cross-checked against TB counts.
- Cache counters: accepted requests, unique hit/miss decisions, installed fills, valid victim evictions, AR/R/AW/W/B handshakes, outstanding-depth histogram and response latency histogram. Do not count a level asserted for several stalled cycles as several cache misses.
- Single-core speedup = baseline ROI cycles / candidate ROI cycles for identical work.
- Multicore throughput = total checked work / global elapsed RTL time; also report each worker's completion latency. Separately report core-count scaling efficiency against the matched one-core case.
- Keep fixed-total-cache and fixed-cache-per-core scaling experiments distinct; this tranche holds cache geometry constant across each A/B pair.
- Generic logic cells, sequential bits, inferred/mapped SRAM dimensions and mapped-cell area are separate quantities. Never interpret `proc/opt/stat` as physical area.
- SMT grant delay, retire shares and shared-resource attribution are initially diagnostic. Weighted speedup/fairness are promoted only after per-hart measurement is validated.

**P1 exit:** nonempty, independently checked measurements; deterministic reruns agree or variability is diagnosed; no speedup is derived from a trace parser or missing counter defaulting to zero.

#### P2 — Implement one optional L2 replacement candidate
**Goal:** make a small capacity-efficiency improvement without changing issue/SMT scheduling or memory concurrency.

1. Add `L2RoundRobinEn` (default zero) to both user and derived configuration structs; copy it through `build_config_pkg`; check enabled L2 geometry and supported ways. Enumerate and update every matching explicit configuration initializer, including relevant fixtures and deprecated packages where applicable. Do not rely on the old documented package count. Keep every checked-in production package at zero.
2. Add a small dedicated `g6lc_l2_replacement.sv` leaf under `corev_apu/l2_cache` so replacement policy does not enlarge the controller's responsibilities. It owns per-set next-way state and exposes invalid-first selection plus a successful-fill update. The legacy path stays structurally separate and stateless when the new option is off.
3. Keep metadata behind the `tc_sram` memory seam. Arrange its one-cycle lookup alongside accepted AR/S_IDLE so the existing S_TAG decision can consume it without adding a demand-hit pipeline stage. Seed the next-way value on every successful installation; prove an all-valid set cannot consult uninitialized metadata after reset. If the SRAM interface/library cannot meet that timing/initialization contract, stop for review rather than silently add a hit cycle or a large flop array.
4. Select the first invalid way if one exists; otherwise use that set's recorded next way. Advance to the successor of the installed way exactly once when tag/data installation succeeds. A repeated lookup, stalled install, bypass, or reset must not falsely advance the pointer. Preserve victim address reporting and invalidation ordering.
5. Expose a `RoundRobinEn` parameter on `g6lc_l2_top` defaulted to zero; bind it from `CVA6Cfg.L2RoundRobinEn` only for the L2 instance in `g6lc_cluster`. Bind the L3 specialization to zero explicitly. No memory-map, cache-size, line-size or L1 replacement change.
6. Add a feature PMU event for successful round-robin policy updates/installs using an unused selector, proposed group 2/index 5 after confirming uniqueness. Thread it through the existing cluster -> ariane (both wrapper branches) -> cva6 -> perf_counters hierarchy. It is a shared-cache event, not a per-hart event; do not sum duplicated per-core views. Tie it to zero when the policy is off and retain all published selectors. Rich experimental histograms remain simulation-only.
7. Register the new leaf in the SoC Makefile source list and every actual consuming manifest, including `verif/sv-timing-tests/flists/full_corev_apu.f`. Do not add uncore IP to the core-only flist just to satisfy an incomplete ingest.
8. Run leaf safety/progress/cover checks, latency/handshake comparison and off-path equivalence before cluster runs.

**Quick size inference:** for 256 KiB, 64-byte lines and eight ways: 512 sets x 3 bits = 1,536 logical replacement bits (192 bytes). This is not physical SRAM area: compiler granularity, peripheral circuits, padding and access power may dominate. Report those costs rather than claiming a 192-byte physical implementation.

**P2 exit:** old/default path is behaviorally and structurally equivalent; candidate is functionally correct on the leaf suite; no new demand-hit stage, latch, clock domain or silent drop; measurements may show a win, a loss or no effect.

#### P3 — Integrate, measure, and decide promotion
1. First compare T=1/N=1 and T=1/N=2 experimental instances derived from the same scalar/stream base with the same L2 geometry. Confirm that the workload actually reaches L2 and does not fit entirely in L1; measure misses and traffic, not labels such as "L3 stream".
2. Keep standard `cv64a6_imafdc_sv39` and `cv32a65x` minimum/default gates. Run stream8 CRT/H-edge controls on the actual candidate build, not a prior executable.
3. Preserve named SMT B/legacy baselines and known-green directed/held-cookie results with unchanged inputs. Maintain known-red natural-boot cases as explicit residuals. Do not substitute a cap exit, different ELF, different execute map, or more peels to keep the headline green.
4. Any candidate-on SMT/L3 or wider-core claim waits for that combination's prerequisites. L3 stays legacy in this tranche; OoO/RVV combinations remain unsupported for a new performance claim until their existing blockers are addressed. Their config/off-path compile checks and recorded residuals are not silently omitted.
5. Add an on/off instrumented-versus-uninstrumented check so observers do not change functional behavior or simulated cycle results. Host simulation speed is a tooling metric only.
6. Run paired leaf synthesis with identical tool version, top, memory treatment and constraints; compare incremental logic/sequential/memory cost. Run the current synth-clean gate separately. Use sv-timing only to screen paths; actual target-frequency/area/energy claims require real library-based synthesis and STA, and power measurements or a declared activity-based estimate.
7. Use a Pareto decision: require checked-work improvement beyond observed variability on the predeclared primary cases, no correctness/stability regression, and no material regression on the declared protected cases. Publish every case, including adverse ones, worst case, geometric mean for normalized speedups, area delta and unavailable power data. Pre-register guardrails before examining candidate results; never tune thresholds after the fact to admit it.
8. If the candidate does not qualify, leave production defaults unchanged and report P3 as incomplete/rejected, with the evidence for the next experiment. Do not silently substitute a broad rewrite or claim a first win from a tailored leaf case. If it qualifies, ship the feature as an explicit optional capability; default-on selection remains a separate product decision.

**First-tranche completion:** trustworthy reusable measurements and evidence selection, accurate affected architecture records, and either a fully evidenced optional replacement improvement or an explicitly blocked/rejected result. A component-level win is not a system-level throughput or tape-out claim.

### Follow-on roadmap — requires a new approval after P3
These are ordered decision gates, not permission to implement the whole roadmap now.

| Track | Next exact mechanism | Prerequisite / why | Primary loci |
|---|---|---|---|
| Multicore correctness | Lossless invalidation admission/reservation, per-target coalescing, selected-source acknowledgments; stable AXI grants under backpressure | A finite queue cannot turn coherence into best effort; more traffic increases exposure | `coherence/g6lc_coherence_hub.sv`, `g6lc_inval_bus.sv`, `g6lc_snoop_filter.sv`, `l3_cache/g6lc_l3_inclusive_inv.sv`, `src/g6lc_cluster.sv` |
| Memory concurrency | Separate request acceptance, tag lookup, miss records/fill state and response delivery; start with two read misses and hit-under-miss | Audit MSHR readiness when a matching line's waiter queue is full; store full waiter geometry/ID; reserve victim ways and handle invalidation-vs-fill; bounded progress and error propagation | `l2_cache/g6lc_l2_{top,mshr,data,tag}.sv`, hub ID/OT mapping |
| Shared memory scaling | Increase outstanding limits, banks and DRAM channels only when measured occupancy/backpressure identifies the limiting stage | Current hub default is four shared OT slots; L2 serial service and way-based banks can dominate; core count does not set DRAM channels | `g6lc_cluster`, coherence hub, `g6lc_ai_dram_backend`, shared xbar |
| Prefetch | Put the prefetched line in a cache or a real prefetch buffer, with reserved response IDs and bounded demand interference | Current absorbed-response probe is not a cache fill; need PMA/MMIO filtering, useful/late/useless metrics and queue pressure control | `l3_cache/g6lc_server_prefetcher.sv`, L2/L3 request/fill seam |
| Single-core | Restore reliable T=1 two-wide issue before I=4; count barriers/RAW/FU conflicts, then remove only proven redundant serialization | One LSU/FPU/multiply path still limits classes of instruction mixes; architectural scoreboard age, not raw PC magnitude, must define dependence/order | `fetch_B/*`, `issue_stage.sv`, `smt_legacy/g6lc_issue_barrier.sv`, `issue_read_operands.sv`, `ex_stage.sv`, `scoreboard.sv`, `commit_stage.sv` |
| Instruction supply | Tune existing predictor sizes and FTQ/FDIP/loop buffering separately | Cache and branch trace evidence plus resolved-target/fetch-byte correctness; preserve frozen A and current B contracts | `frontend/g6lc_bp_*`, `fetch_B/*`, `g6lc_icache.sv` |
| SMT completion | Per-hart FPR, translation/root/ASID/PMP provenance, trap/CSR/flags/counter ownership, reservation identity; natural boot before removing holds | OS context switching cannot repair unbanked state across invisible hardware fetch switches; shared physical structures need explicit owner tags | `g6lc_smt_csr_bank`, `g6lc_smt_regfile`, IRO, LSU/MMU, commit/CSR, perf |
| SMT efficiency | Tune ready-thread allocation/grant policy and queue quotas, then idle-bank gating | Define conditional fairness under bounded memory service and hold states; do not interpret `SmtStarveLimit` as an unconditional time bound | `g6lc_thread_select`, `g6lc_hart_state`, scoreboard/issue and clock-gating seams |
| Slice scheduling | Qualify real address-slice formation, WB tracking and wrap-safe age before enabling | Current path accepts/issues port zero and reduces WB completion; published literature gains are only priors | `g6lc_slice_{dispatch,steer,ist,iq,rmt}.sv` |
| OoO | Begin T=1/I=2: committed/speculative maps, tagged branch checkpoints, accepted-producer/result-ready wakeup, byte/age-correct LSQ, precise stores/traps; then retirement width | Reproduce self-blocking stores, checkpoint/flush restore and forwarding concerns; do not start with 4c x SMT2 | `ooo/g6lc_{rename,prf,iq,rob,lsq,memdep,ooo_dispatch}.sv`, IRO/scoreboard/commit |
| Physical optimization | Tag/predictor/PRF macro mapping, port/bank choices, selective clock gating | Same semantics and declared latency; actual memory compiler and STA evidence, not generic cell counts | `g6lc_l2_tag`, predictor tables, PRF, tech-cell seam |

For each track, use the smallest counterexample or changed bottleneck metric to justify proceeding. Global "optimality" is not claimed; the program produces a measured Pareto frontier over the qualified search space.

### QEMU and rapid inference
- Use existing `g6q gen` model/matrix, `conform`, software execution and D1 comparison as early diagnostics. Ingest the complete SoC manifest and effective experimental configuration. A flist membership result proves presence, not dynamic implementation completeness.
- Do not add a CPU cycle model or an RTL checkpoint importer in this tranche. Do not turn QEMU PMU zeroes into no-stall/no-miss claims. Leave `evidence:false` intact.
- Raw instruction/branch/memory traces can shortlist later cache/predictor candidates. RTL timing and wrong-path effects calibrate the shortlist. The existing `perf-model` is an earlier upstream control model, not automatically valid for current SMT/OoO.
- Candidate-priority heuristic: observed blocked work x fraction the proposed mechanism can remove, divided by estimated added hardware and verification burden. Treat it as a sorting prior, not a performance prediction. Amdahl bounds and `bandwidth * latency / line_bytes` reject implausible claims before RTL work.
- Avoid a Cartesian sweep. First hold topology/geometry/ISA constant and change one mechanism. Then check interactions with held-out patterns and the named stability envelopes. Reuse one parameterized leaf binary where only input traffic changes; rebuild only for actual elaboration changes.

### Proactive architectural maintenance
At P0 and in every implementation change, update affected existing records using one evidence tuple: source/build identity, effective configuration, invariant, mechanism status, test envelope, actual verdict, remaining gap, timing/area impact and software visibility. Do not create another roadmap or rewrite the historical logs.

Required targets:
- `architecture/current-stage.md`: concise current envelope/status truth, no inherited Linux or production claim without artifacts.
- `architecture/l2-l3-cache/README.md`, `agents/guides/AGENTS-l2l3-cache.md`: serial service versus future concurrency, actual bank mapping, replacement contract, lookup/install timing and PMU units.
- `architecture/multi-core/README.md`, `architecture/multi-threading/README.md`, `smt2-product-closeout.md`: stable versus residual combinations, ownership prerequisites, service-core reservation and conditional fairness.
- `architecture/router-core-upgrade-program.md`, `remaining-upgrade-sequence.md`: refresh only relevant status/next-work rows, clearly distinguish priors from measurements.
- `AGENTS-specs-to-impl.md`/`AGENTS-specs-to-tests.md` and derived `AGENTS-specs-coverage.md`/`agents/spec/INDEX.md` when their semantics/suite mappings are affected.
- `AGENTS-dts-validation.md`: record that replacement alone changes neither topology nor ISA; test-local bare-metal topology overrides are not Linux DT claims. Change actual DTS only for an independently approved software-visible configuration.
- `AGENTS-todo.md`: track every residual, acceptance gate, rejected hypothesis and follow-up. Preserve the user's ongoing APU rows.

### Files to Modify
#### First tranche, existing files
- `build-platform/src/config/{schema,defaults,load}.ts`: additive qualification/result configuration, validation and suite mapping.
- `build-platform/src/tests/runner.ts`, `src/cli/commands/verify.ts`: evidence-aware results and strict qualification; preserve existing developer behavior.
- `build-platform/test/{config,regress,shell-bash}.test.ts` where appropriate: selection, false-PASS and skip behavior; add a focused new test file only if these cannot express pure result/manifest tests cleanly.
- `verif/regress/remote/testharness_proxy.py`: actual-input build identity, build/run manifest checks and terminal-result separation; merge against current user changes.
- `verif/regress/soft-ladder-build-harness.sh`: explicit isolated config/flist overlay and post-bootrom-generation build stamp; defaults unchanged.
- `verif/regress/{stability-regress,ooo-l3-tests}.sh`: qualification-aware child evidence without globally deleting developer fallbacks.
- `core/ooo/formal/g6lc_ooo_rename_props.sv`, `g6lc_ooo_rename.sby`: legal harness geometry, constraints and separate cover checks; no broad DUT repair.

- `corev_apu/l2_cache/g6lc_l2_top.sv`, `corev_apu/l3_cache/g6lc_l3_top.sv`, `corev_apu/src/g6lc_cluster.sv`: narrow replacement integration and L3 isolation.
- `corev_apu/src/ariane.sv`, `core/cva6.sv`, `core/perf_counters.sv`: feature-event plumbing only, zero when disabled; update direct instantiations discovered by the port change.
- `corev_apu/tb/ariane_testharness.sv`: opt-in simulation-only ROI/provenance/metrics wiring; no functional core changes.

- Affected existing architecture/guide/traceability documents listed above.

#### Necessary new files, to be created only after approval
- `corev_apu/l2_cache/g6lc_l2_replacement.sv`: one-purpose optional metadata/selection leaf.
- `verif/tb/l2/tb_g6lc_l2_replacement.sv`: real-cache A/B, reference data and deterministic traffic/latency checks.
- `verif/tb/l2/g6lc_l2_replacement_props.sv` and `.sby`: bounded safety, off-path/initialization obligations and non-vacuous covers.
- `verif/regress/l2-replacement.py` plus thin `.sh` gateway: standalone stdlib-only manifest, remote dispatch, metrics and positive/negative result validation, modeled after existing remote leaf suites without AI dependencies.

Generated overlays, flists, manifests, results and timing outputs live only in ignored build/run workspaces. No new project documentation file, external dependency, or tool-specific configuration is required.

### Verification
All commands below are implementation-time checks, not runs performed during planning.

- [ ] Run host `bun run typecheck` and `bun test` from `build-platform`; include synthetic missing/failing/skipped/mismatched-target/stale-cache/truncated-log cases. (Continuation: qualification tests now cover AMO-arith markers, `l2-equiv` classifier, and isolated overlay allowlist.)
- [ ] Run new runner self-tests and positive/negative terminal-marker tests before remote dispatch. (`l2_leaf_passed` requires `+amo-arith`; `l2_equiv_passed` requires EQUIVALENCE PASS and rejects ladder fail.)
- [ ] Prove non-reset cover reachability separately from bounded assertion checks; use live modules and independent reference obligations.
- [ ] Use proxy `py` for leaf RTL runs and the existing `build`, `run`, `di`, `soak` commands for core/SMT qualification; acquire the remote execution slot and preserve active user runs.
- [ ] Run target-correct lint, strict elaboration, formal, simulation and synth-clean gates after each RTL change, satisfying `build.sh/build.ps1 verify` obligations through the approved remote execution path. Do not assume the existing local `verify` implementation itself routes Verilator remotely.
- [ ] Preserve minimum/default configs, stream CRT and H-edge controls, and named SMT oracle/held-cookie controls. Record unavailable or existing-red configurations explicitly; they cannot earn release status from a cap exit.
- [ ] Compare candidate off against the frozen legacy cache behavior; verify new state/event signals const-fold away. Compare normalized netlists or equivalence, not file-byte identity after adding a config field.
- [ ] Collect unique-event counts, checked work and ROI cycles under identical geometry/latency/seed; reject zero-work or missing-result cases.
- [ ] Check adverse/held-out replacement patterns, all supported tested geometries and reset/invalidation/backpressure/error interactions before integration.
- [ ] Separate synthesis smoke, mapped leaf logic, SRAM granularity and physical area. Screen timing with sv-timing; require real STA before target-frequency closure claims.
- [ ] If QEMU code must change to ingest the new field correctly, run `python tools/g6q.py check` and retain its independent/std-only/read-only/GPL-out boundaries; otherwise use it unchanged as a diagnostic.
- [ ] Update architecture and traceability in lockstep; run applicable config/DTS and licensing checks. Never edit upstream attribution or existing comments as incidental cleanup.

### Risks / Stop conditions
- Required licensing config files are present and active contributor is Etienne Cimon. Only CLA templates were located under `CLA/`; verify the required tier-R authorization record before any tier-R implementation. Do not invent a waiver or modify policy to bypass it.
- Baseline correctness may block multicore or SMT promotion. Leaf research can continue independently, but first-win qualification stays incomplete until its relevant gates pass.
- Round-robin may not win on the selected workload mix or physical SRAM implementation. No default-on change and no replacement experiment expansion without reviewing the result.
- A shared-cache policy changes timing even with untouched SMT RTL. Candidate-on SMT claims need the real paired controls; default-off equivalence is not candidate-on correctness.
- Metadata initialization/one-cycle-read feasibility must be established; do not hide a new hit cycle or macro-area overhead.
- Current proxy/harness scripts have broad source-sync and stale-object assumptions. Use explicit immutable snapshots and respect concurrent user work; destructive cleanup requires separate confirmation.
- Full OoO repair, wide retirement, nonblocking L2, replacement of prefetch architecture, SMT architectural banking, RVV integration, APU firmware placement, broad QEMU D2 modeling and full physical sign-off are deliberately outside this first approval.

**Inferred P0–P4 ranking (best area/perf first):**

1. **Keep `L2RoundRobinEn=0`.** Saves 1 `$mem_v2` and ~24 generic cells. Core cycle delta is 0. 8-way leaf mix is ~0.7% (hot_scan win cancelled by `protected_hot`). This is the area win.
2. **Do not turn RR on for SMT2.** Isolated RR-on livelocks (I=2 fetch). Negative correctness.
3. **Leave MSHR waiters unwired.** Units leaf PASS; serialized top `mshr_full`/`bank_conf` stay 0. Wiring them is nonblocking-L2 follow-on.
4. **Cluster AMOCAS / stream minis** stay functional controls (0 cycle delta), not ROI.
5. **Rename covers** are OoO non-vacuity, not L2 area.
6. RR at the L2 pin only pays if a client bypasses L1; the core path does not.

Do not default-on. Do not merge packages. Do not wire MSHR waiters or repair SMT2 fetch without a new approval. **Performance promotion is still NOT QUALIFIED.**
